# Spaces control bindings

Each entry identifies the observed or explicitly supplemental control, its interaction record and any target-specific clarification. Labels grouped in one paragraph share that precise record. Shared chrome is listed where it appears; it never creates a second artifact owner. Source ordinal numbers help locate the inspected static occurrence, and are not production IDs.

<a id="h.htkvwduir195"></a>

#0 Model picker; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). Open the shared picker; role, effort, context and selection mechanics use [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21)–010.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 New Chat → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Create one scoped conversation; Study retains selected subject/topic and source policy.

#20 Chat Groups → [IX-SPACES-016](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.kt35lgbcln2v). Open canonical Group/Project containers, not Multi-Agent chat or another Space.

#21 Specialists → [IX-SPACES-051](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fo6pd25gptxi). Open the Specialist catalogue with originating composer/return context.

#22 A calmer study week; #23 Plan next week; #24 Compare two approaches; #25 A place for every idea; #26 My presentation outline → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Resume the captured ConversationID/work reference; sample label and shared prototype route are not identity.

#27 Summary → [IX-SPACES-066](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.y6wotie9sitg). Disclosure expands existing work view; explicit navigation link opens selected task summary without creating work.

#28 Review the plan; #29 Make it lighter → [IX-SPACES-030](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.8b5udrcgp0km). Apply the labelled follow-up to its exact plan/proposal/artifact context; review does not itself approve a mutation.

#30 Copy response → [IX-SPACES-031](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ud0ujord1tq8). Copy this captured response, not a later last message.

#31 Read aloud → [IX-SPACES-032](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.k97ghycgspil). Speak this response through the actual eligible voice route; do not start microphone capture.

#32 Retry response → [IX-SPACES-033](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.75yo7aoklodw). Retry the selected response with preserved branch/source policy and retained original result.

#33 More message actions → [IX-SPACES-039](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7irt2gevi0pj). Open applicable message actions for the captured message; do not run the first item automatically.

#34 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#35 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#36 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.

#37 Main → [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21). Select the main settings role only; preserve per-role selection and start no capture.

#38 Voice → [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21). Select the voice settings role only; preserve per-role selection and start no capture.

#39 Vision → [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21). Select the vision settings role only; preserve per-role selection and start no capture.

#40 Reasoning level → [IX-SPACES-004](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.q2t12r8epkzj). Requested effort range 0–100, step 10; truthful effective provider mapping remains separate.

#41 Context8% used; #44 ModelQwen 4; #50 Assistant/SpecialistNone → [IX-SPACES-003](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.siz5fq49sdyc). Independent Context/Model/Assistant-Specialist disclosure, not a model/participant selection.

#42 ; #43 Apply and reload → [IX-SPACES-006](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.4odanvh8hssf). Draft positive integer context limit; only explicit Apply and reload commits, never Enter in field/blur.

#45 Search main models → [IX-SPACES-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ydo82pmvw7b5). Filter only the active role’s candidates and preserve hidden selected identity.

#46 Qwen 4This device; #47 Qwen 4Home PC; #48 ChatGPT 6 SolCloud → [IX-SPACES-008](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fkb723jci8ev). Select this actual model/route for the active role; does not install/load/admit work.

#49 Browse models → [IX-SPACES-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.82uzd18m5hjr). Open Home model catalogue with role/return context and retained pending context draft.

#51 Search assistants and specialists; #52 None; #53 AlexPlanning & day-to-day work; #54 MayaWriting & careful editing; #55 RowanResearch & source checking; #56 ProofreaderClearer writing, with your meaning intact.; #57 Exam TutorPractice tied to your subject and specification.; #58 Code ReviewerReview a diff and explain the important changes.; #59 Source CheckerTrace a claim to its source and flag missing evidence.; #60 Data AnalystExplore a dataset and explain its results.; #61 Presentation EditorImprove the story, pacing and layout of a deck. → [IX-SPACES-010](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.hngyah2gha8k). Search/select active-role participant; None clears only this role binding without granting scope.

Resume the captured ConversationID/work reference; sample label and shared prototype route are not identity.

#27 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-133](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.oiggjnb69523). Open options anchored to the captured conversation/workspace; no immediate action.

#28 Message draft → [IX-SPACES-018](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.bai4aclewxqc). Edit unsent draft with retained selection/IME; no implicit send.

#29 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#30 Text formatting; #31 Insert emoji → [IX-SPACES-019](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.6hdqefnhcdxv). Formatting/emoji uses retained draft caret; category/search is presentation and emoji inserts actual Unicode.

#32 Conversation permissions; #34 Close conversation permissions; #35 Close conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#33 Send message → [IX-SPACES-025](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.nd3nsji6z29j). Admit the current ready draft once and await actual acknowledgement; shelf browsing uses [IX-SPACES-024](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.vaxct2x5tchq).

#36 Attached sources permission; #37 Planner permission; #38 Boards permission; #39 Web research permission; #40 External messages permission; #41 Other Assistant memory permission → [IX-SPACES-041](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.dygi9mq86h1g). Use exact row choices Allow reading, Ask each time and Blocked; disabled rows remain explanatory, not granted.

#42 Manage app permissions; #43 Cancel; #44 Save for conversation → [IX-SPACES-042](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.jvwlpu50zd8i). Manage opens the shared registry; Save commits exact scoped draft; Cancel discards only permission edits.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 New Chat → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Create one scoped conversation; Study retains selected subject/topic and source policy.

#20 Chat Groups → [IX-SPACES-016](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.kt35lgbcln2v). Open canonical Group/Project containers, not Multi-Agent chat or another Space.

#21 Specialists → [IX-SPACES-051](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fo6pd25gptxi). Open the Specialist catalogue with originating composer/return context.

#22 A calmer study week; #23 Plan next week; #24 Compare two approaches; #25 A place for every idea; #26 My presentation outline → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Resume the captured ConversationID/work reference; sample label and shared prototype route are not identity.

#27 Summary → [IX-SPACES-066](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.y6wotie9sitg). Disclosure expands existing work view; explicit navigation link opens selected task summary without creating work.

#28 Review the plan; #29 Make it lighter → [IX-SPACES-030](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.8b5udrcgp0km). Apply the labelled follow-up to its exact plan/proposal/artifact context; review does not itself approve a mutation.

#30 Copy response → [IX-SPACES-031](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ud0ujord1tq8). Copy this captured response, not a later last message.

#31 Read aloud → [IX-SPACES-032](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.k97ghycgspil). Speak this response through the actual eligible voice route; do not start microphone capture.

#32 Retry response → [IX-SPACES-033](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.75yo7aoklodw). Retry the selected response with preserved branch/source policy and retained original result.

#33 More message actions → [IX-SPACES-039](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7irt2gevi0pj). Open applicable message actions for the captured message; do not run the first item automatically.

#34 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#35 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#36 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.

#27 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-133](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.oiggjnb69523). Open options anchored to the captured conversation/workspace; no immediate action.

#28 Close attachments; #35 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#29 FilesLocal files and your Library; #30 SkillsInstructions for a task; #31 Plugins & AppsConnected capabilities; #32 SpecialistsReusable expertise; #33 AssistantsPersistent collaborators → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open this typed picker; selected reference must become ready/authorised before admission, not automatically invoke it.

#34 Message draft → [IX-SPACES-018](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.bai4aclewxqc). Edit unsent draft with retained selection/IME; no implicit send.

#36 Text formatting; #37 Insert emoji → [IX-SPACES-019](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.6hdqefnhcdxv). Formatting/emoji uses retained draft caret; category/search is presentation and emoji inserts actual Unicode.

#38 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#39 Send message → [IX-SPACES-025](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.nd3nsji6z29j). Admit the current ready draft once and await actual acknowledgement; shelf browsing uses [IX-SPACES-024](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.vaxct2x5tchq).

#0 Run summary and approval; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). #13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#19 New Chat → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Create one scoped conversation; Study retains selected subject/topic and source policy.

#20 Chat Groups → [IX-SPACES-016](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.kt35lgbcln2v). Open canonical Group/Project containers, not Multi-Agent chat or another Space.

#21 Specialists → [IX-SPACES-051](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fo6pd25gptxi). Open the Specialist catalogue with originating composer/return context.

#22 A calmer study week; #23 Plan next week●; #24 Compare two approaches; #25 A place for every idea; #26 My presentation outline → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Resume the captured ConversationID/work reference; sample label and shared prototype route are not identity.

#27 Decline; #28 Approve 3 additions → [IX-SPACES-073](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.cdtu577ecj36). Decline rejects the exact pending proposal; Approve reviews/applies only the displayed actual additions, not unrelated work.

#29 Summary → [IX-SPACES-066](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.y6wotie9sitg). Disclosure expands existing work view; explicit navigation link opens selected task summary without creating work.

#30 View Action Graph → [IX-SPACES-066](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.y6wotie9sitg). Open this runtime Action Graph, distinct from Present authoring Actions.

#31 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#32 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#33 Stop current task → [IX-SPACES-028](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.sviu14w774ix). Request cancellation of the current TaskID; await actual state and preserve committed effects.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). #8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

Open/close the specific assistants/workspace pane, retaining view state; backdrop/close dismisses only its pane.

#20 My Assistants; #21 AlexMain conversation; #22 MayaMain conversation; #23 RowanMain conversation; #26 Create Assistant → [IX-SPACES-050](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.unc8nw6oc9nb). Open catalogue/create definition or the named canonical Main conversation as labelled.

#25 Teams → [IX-SPACES-056](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.tqos69f1tgjd). Open canonical teams/membership; no inferred invitation or private-memory sharing.

#27 Runs & approvals → [IX-SPACES-067](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gwjdgvu9bwqk). Open Actions filtered to this Assistant/scope and pending work/reviews; opening does not approve.

#28 Shared sources → [IX-SPACES-055](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.lprk24uejwz9). Inspect the selected authorised shared source collection without exposing private memory.

#31 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-053](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.22jvk5ne5e49). Open the exact scoped menu; Assistant identity-row menu is Configure/Conversation permissions, not generic conversation commands.

#32 Configure; #43 Configure Maya → [IX-SPACES-053](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.22jvk5ne5e49). Open this Assistant’s canonical definition with return context, not conversation permission edits.

#33 Conversation permissions; #40 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#34 Review in Write → [IX-SPACES-030](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.8b5udrcgp0km). Apply the labelled follow-up to its exact plan/proposal/artifact context; review does not itself approve a mutation.

#35 Copy response → [IX-SPACES-031](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ud0ujord1tq8). Copy this captured response, not a later last message.

#36 Read aloud → [IX-SPACES-032](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.k97ghycgspil). Speak this response through the actual eligible voice route; do not start microphone capture.

#37 Retry response → [IX-SPACES-033](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.75yo7aoklodw). Retry the selected response with preserved branch/source policy and retained original result.

#38 More message actions → [IX-SPACES-039](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7irt2gevi0pj). Open applicable message actions for the captured message; do not run the first item automatically.

#39 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#41 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.

#45 Essay structure; #46 Tone and clarity → [IX-SPACES-054](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ey4v142zpmad). Resume this Assistant’s exact secondary ConversationID while preserving separate Main identity.

#0 Rowan Main; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). Open the shared picker; role, effort, context and selection mechanics use [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21)–010.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#12 Assistants → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Assistants destination and restore its actual work context.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 Close assistants; #24 Workspace; #29 Show assistants; #30 Show workspace; #42 Close workspace; #44 Close workspace; #47 Close assistants → [IX-SPACES-052](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.85rfznudvfg4). Open/close the specific assistants/workspace pane, retaining view state; backdrop/close dismisses only its pane.

#20 My Assistants; #21 AlexMain conversation; #22 MayaMain conversation; #23 RowanMain conversation; #26 Create Assistant → [IX-SPACES-050](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.unc8nw6oc9nb). Open catalogue/create definition or the named canonical Main conversation as labelled.

#25 Teams → [IX-SPACES-056](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.tqos69f1tgjd). Open canonical teams/membership; no inferred invitation or private-memory sharing.

#27 Runs & approvals → [IX-SPACES-067](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gwjdgvu9bwqk). Open Actions filtered to this Assistant/scope and pending work/reviews; opening does not approve.

#28 Shared sources → [IX-SPACES-055](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.lprk24uejwz9). Inspect the selected authorised shared source collection without exposing private memory.

#31 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-053](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.22jvk5ne5e49). Open the exact scoped menu; Assistant identity-row menu is Configure/Conversation permissions, not generic conversation commands.

#32 Configure; #43 Configure Rowan → [IX-SPACES-053](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.22jvk5ne5e49). Open this Assistant’s canonical definition with return context, not conversation permission edits.

#33 Conversation permissions; #40 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#34 Review source scope → [IX-SPACES-053](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.22jvk5ne5e49). Inspect supplied/bound sources and effective permission scope; no automatic broadening.

#35 Copy response → [IX-SPACES-031](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ud0ujord1tq8). Copy this captured response, not a later last message.

#36 Read aloud → [IX-SPACES-032](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.k97ghycgspil). Speak this response through the actual eligible voice route; do not start microphone capture.

#37 Retry response → [IX-SPACES-033](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.75yo7aoklodw). Retry the selected response with preserved branch/source policy and retained original result.

#38 More message actions → [IX-SPACES-039](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7irt2gevi0pj). Open applicable message actions for the captured message; do not run the first item automatically.

#39 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#41 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.

#45 Source comparison; #46 Research questions → [IX-SPACES-054](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ey4v142zpmad). Resume this Assistant’s exact secondary ConversationID while preserving separate Main identity.

#31 Your answer and working → [IX-SPACES-101](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.dw2plmzc0j88). Edit the current question’s answer/working draft; preserve revisions and do not submit on newline.

#32 Show a hint; #33 Check answer → [IX-SPACES-102](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.adt48yangmb2). Hint records actual assistance; Check answer explicitly freezes and submits one attempt for real evaluation.

#35  → [IX-SPACES-103](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.19ike46t5qfg). Subjective confidence: Not sure; independent from assessed proficiency. The empty extracted label must receive an accessible group/value name.

#36  → [IX-SPACES-103](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.19ike46t5qfg). Subjective confidence: Fairly sure; independent from assessed proficiency. The empty extracted label must receive an accessible group/value name.

#37  → [IX-SPACES-103](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.19ike46t5qfg). Subjective confidence: Very sure; independent from assessed proficiency. The empty extracted label must receive an accessible group/value name.

#38 Guided explanations and worked examples. Visible mode Learn; explanatory title is description, not sole accessible name. Applies to next workflow without sending/resetting.

#39 Hints are available. An attempt is recorded only after you submit an answer for checking. → [IX-SPACES-099](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.o01lksyi9hm8). Visible mode Practise; explanatory title is description, not sole accessible name. Applies to next workflow without sending/resetting.

#40 Review the Forms assessment rules and marking rubric before starting. Visible mode Test; explanatory title is description, not sole accessible name. Applies to next workflow without sending/resetting.

#41 Retrieve earlier learning and review due topics. Opening notes or flipping a card does not count as an answer. → [IX-SPACES-099](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.o01lksyi9hm8). Visible mode Review; explanatory title is description, not sole accessible name. Applies to next workflow without sending/resetting.

Select factual source policy subject to governing binding; web/general knowledge/memory remain independent.

#44 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#45 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#46 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). #0 Plan, sources and outputs; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). Open the shared picker; role, effort, context and selection mechanics use [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21)–010.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#12 Assistants → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Assistants destination and restore its actual work context.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 New research; #20 My research; #21 Overview; #22 Sources; #23 Findings & notes; #24 Outputs; #25 A low-energy workspace; #26 Compare study methods → [IX-SPACES-076](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.36fv076x8850). Open/create the exact research workspace/section or saved ResearchID; route fixture is not a reset.

#27 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-133](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.oiggjnb69523). Open options anchored to the captured conversation/workspace; no immediate action.

#28 Review plan → [IX-SPACES-078](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.x8kqj5ld0d9r). Review this research plan/revision and actual budget before its separate start command.

#29 Quick; #30 Extended; #31 Deep; #32 Custom → [IX-SPACES-077](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e7igt2dxbxjp). Choose the research depth/budget policy; Custom exposes explicit bounded fields, not hidden provider promises.

#33 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#34 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#35 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.

#0 Conversational localisation; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). Open the shared picker; role, effort, context and selection mechanics use [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21)–010.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#12 Assistants → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Assistants destination and restore its actual work context.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 New translation; #20 Translation sets; #21 Glossaries; #22 Welcome message; #23 Project overview; #29 Add target language → [IX-SPACES-086](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.4psugfm4yh61). Open/create the exact TranslationSet/glossary or add a target locale draft; no standalone Translate app.

#24 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-133](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.oiggjnb69523). Open options anchored to the captured conversation/workspace; no immediate action.

#25 Copy French variant; #26 Refine French variant; #27 Copy German variant; #28 Refine German variant → [IX-SPACES-087](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.x4sa14oj4akt). Copy/refine this specific locale variant and preserve sibling variants/source revision.

#30 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#31 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#32 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.

#0 Presentation work in progress; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). Open the shared picker; role, effort, context and selection mechanics use [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21)–010.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Shopping → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Shopping destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 New Chat → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Create one scoped conversation; Study retains selected subject/topic and source policy.

#20 Chat Groups → [IX-SPACES-016](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.kt35lgbcln2v). Open canonical Group/Project containers, not Multi-Agent chat or another Space.

#21 Specialists → [IX-SPACES-051](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fo6pd25gptxi). Open the Specialist catalogue with originating composer/return context.

#22 A calmer study week; #23 Revision materials · Presentation●; #24 Compare two approaches; #25 A place for every idea; #26 My presentation outline → [IX-SPACES-014](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.fvz2br1f1z8s). Resume the captured ConversationID/work reference; sample label and shared prototype route are not identity.

#27 People working here; #29 View agents → [IX-SPACES-057](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.xilxalbm8y62). Inspect the real participating child/run or its actual output; each View work row retains its own target.

#28 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-133](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.oiggjnb69523). Open options anchored to the captured conversation/workspace; no immediate action.

#30 Open in Present; #31 Preview zoom out; #32 Preview zoom in; #33 Fit preview → [IX-SPACES-060](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.tawkkeizx7pe). Operate the actual provisional preview/canonical output; zoom/fit is viewer state and never a content mutation.

#34 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#35 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#36 Stop current task → [IX-SPACES-028](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.sviu14w774ix). Request cancellation of the current TaskID; await actual state and preserve committed effects.

#0 Experiences; #1 Study → [IX-HOST-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.aseep3t4mi4o). Activate this existing TabID; do not create a new conversation from the fixture destination.

#2 New tab; #3 Search tabs → [IX-HOST-007](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2lu0erxskeje). Create New Tab or search existing tabs, respectively.

#4 Back → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Back acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#5 Forward → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Forward acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#6 Reload → [IX-HOST-009](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.xu84phecojhm). Reload acts on this hosted view and its retained navigation/request state.

#7 Choose a model → [IX-SPACES-001](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.rf8587emfxvz). Open the shared picker; role, effort, context and selection mechanics use [IX-SPACES-002](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.e043wipvv21)–010.

#8 Notifications → [IX-HOST-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.2xlp3zfzez5d). Open shared notifications for the current identity without acknowledging unrelated items.

#9 Jacob · Account menu → [IX-HOST-013](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1DoTJEvTbrzXWYvFu2XLswItDW1sIYbQ-7Mw7mkHt8Pk/edit?tab=t.j832uc9l5tk#heading=h.gp7jnqasyfuz). Open current-profile account menu; displayed fixture name is not fixed product identity.

#10 Chat → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Chat destination and restore its actual work context.

#11 Actions (Atlas legacy label: Tasks) → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Direct Space navigation. Legacy Tasks label resolves to canonical Actions with the existing spaces-running ScreenID.

#12 Assistants → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Assistants destination and restore its actual work context.

#13 Study → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Study destination and restore its actual work context.

#14 Research → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Research destination and restore its actual work context.

#15 Experiences → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Experiences destination and restore its actual work context.

#16 Translate → [IX-SPACES-011](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.r8vpdlkn3wev). Open the Translate destination and restore its actual work context.

#17 Browse Spaces; #18 Space settings → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Explicit Space discovery/management entry, distinct from ordinary rail navigation.

#19 Experiences → [IX-SPACES-012](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7q1fkh1tvis). Open explicit Experience Space selection/management rather than resetting the current experience.

#20 Experience Space options; #21 New experience; #22 My experiences; #23 The lighthouse letters; #26 Sources & rules; #27 A garden on the moon; #28 The makers’ workshop; #29 Story starters; #30 Worlds & scenarios → [IX-SPACES-092](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.asf9zhlia1u8). Target the selected experience/scenario/source-rules context; inspection and starting are distinct.

#24 Checkpoints; #25 Branches; #43 Branch from here → [IX-SPACES-095](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.4se8vv7pew8l). Inspect/branch from the exact experience checkpoint while preserving original state and external receipts.

#31 Summary; #32 Action Graph → [IX-SPACES-094](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.53i8ykoyclfj). Inspect current experience/run state without changing story/world revision.

#33 Conversation options → [IX-SPACES-133](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.oiggjnb69523). Open options anchored to the captured conversation/workspace; no immediate action.

#34 Ask about the path; #35 Check the tide times → [IX-SPACES-093](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.sw20trhdtq24). Commit this available choice against the current world/branch revision once; retain actual consequences and truthful source needs.

#36 Copy response → [IX-SPACES-031](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.ud0ujord1tq8). Copy this captured response, not a later last message.

#37 Read aloud → [IX-SPACES-032](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.k97ghycgspil). Speak this response through the actual eligible voice route; do not start microphone capture.

#38 Retry response → [IX-SPACES-033](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.75yo7aoklodw). Retry the selected response with preserved branch/source policy and retained original result.

#39 Message actions → [IX-SPACES-039](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.7irt2gevi0pj). Open applicable message actions for the captured message; do not run the first item automatically.

#40 Attach to conversation → [IX-SPACES-020](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.gn4bqu6fyedg). Open/dismiss typed attachment choices for the same composer without discarding its draft.

#41 Conversation permissions → [IX-SPACES-040](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.otk7r5s57h9c). Open or dismiss current conversation permission draft; closing is not saving.

#42 Start voice conversation → [IX-SPACES-029](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1CJf0VXUVLXn1qyj3AYUTOTdxEouxuJfqXc48_jHpApE/edit?tab=t.4u1kzupu761c#heading=h.9mk0v3vx9caa). Open the shared source-aware Vision & Voice session entry; its explicit Start begins capture. Browsing the action shelf never starts capture.
